## Sri Lanka added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Sri Lanka's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs in two passes — **94 files / 66 distinct works: 26 pupil textbooks and 40 teacher guides, all Sri Lanka Educational Publications Department (EPD) / National Institute of Education (NIE), in the country's three media — English, Sinhala and Tamil** — and placed 479 items: 289 from the pupil books and 190 teacher-evidence items (the secondary guides at one item per term of the guide's own lesson sequence; the primary guides at one item per syllabus competency group or per part). Pass 1 read the expansion map's 59 files (32 works); Pass 2 pinned 35 further in-hand files the map's subject filter had missed — 34 NIE teacher guides for Grades 1–13 and one pupil textbook (Book S26, filed under the subject "Science for Technology") — and read every one. In the sections below the pupil books are cited as Books S1–S26 and the guides as Books T1–T40. Sri Lanka COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure only on the strength of its pupil-book rows; its guide rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — a guide states its syllabus's competencies, its lesson sequence and its teaching instructions — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent for guide rows). Set facts carried exactly: (1) the Grade 9 Sinhala pupil book is held only as chapter files of two different printings (Books S9–S10) whose shared chapter 20 is a different chapter in each, and NO chapter-26 file exists in either split — the English twin's chapter 26 is Algebraic fractions; the hole is carried as a set gap, not as read; (2) the 2018 Sinhala Grade 9 teacher guide is filed twice (two scans of one work) and is placed once (Book T2), and the file held as the Grade 7 2008 manual's "Tamil" copy is byte-identical to the English manual (same git blob SHA) and is cited once, as the English manual (Book T10); (3) the year-less Tamil Grade 4 and Grade 5 guides are proved by byte size to be NIE's 2015 editions, not the far larger 2019/2020 scans NIE also lists (Books T39–T40); (4) the 1976 Grade 12 Mathematics Part I scan (Book S23) ends with some six pages of an unrelated German textbook's verb tables (PDF pp. 271–276) — its mathematics runs to PDF p. 270 and its last chapters were sampled, not contents-verified; (5) the Grade 1 Sinhala 2026 guide is in hand as a Term 1 volume only (Book T33) — its Terms 2–3 are stocked nowhere — and the Grade 1 Tamil 2016 guide (Book T31) is marked DRAFT on its cover and is placed as filed; (6) the Grade 11 guide's Term 2 table labels one lesson (competency 23.6, inside the geometry cluster) "Central limit theorem" — recorded as printed in Book T19's entry; no row in this course rests on that label; (7) Grade 10 Mathematics Part I (English) and Grade 7 Mathematics Part I (Tamil, 2007–2015 edition) are NOT in hand in any filing — library gaps, not unread books; their twins in the other medium are held and read (Books S17 and S13/S14). The placements' named exclusions — revision exercises, answers, glossaries, assessment apparatus, and the guides' specimen lesson plans and school-based assessment instruments beyond their sequences — are stocked nowhere. Sri Lanka's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand book (94/94 files) has been read and placed, on the in-hand definition.

## Colombia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Colombia's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **27 distinct works: 26 student books and 1 teacher guide, all Colombia Ministerio de Educación Nacional (MEN), in Spanish** — Escuela Nueva Matemáticas cartillas Grades 1–5 (11), Nivelemos Matemáticas student workbooks Grades 1–4 (4), Aulas sin fronteras Grade 6 Unidad 1 student book and its teacher guide (2), Postprimaria student guides Grades 6–9 (4), Secundaria Activa student modules Grades 6–9 (4), and Vamos a Aprender Matemáticas 10 and 11 (2) — 3,928 PDF pages — and placed 293 teaching items: 288 from the student books and 5 teacher-evidence items from the one teacher guide (Book 23). SET CORRECTIONS carried exactly: (1) the expansion map's “28 student + 1 teacher = 29” over-counts by 2 — textbooks.tsv carries duplicate records of Escuela Nueva Grado 1 Cartilla 1 and Grado 2 Cartilla 1 (old blob/main rows for the same two works that also landed as release assets); the distinct set is 27 works and the duplicates are cited once (the library records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records); (2) the set in hand contains NO Grade 3 Cartilla 3 — Grade 3 holds Cartillas 1–2 plus the Nivelemos Grade 3 workbook, and Cartilla 2 completes Unidades 6–9; nothing in hand is a mislabelled Cartilla 3, and no never-published ruling is needed or made; (3) Nivelemos Matemáticas Grado 1 (Book 10) has NO asset in any GitHub release — it was read from the MEN source file itself, landed at exactly the census size (5,020,415 bytes) with EOF intact; the library side still owes that file a GitHub landing. The two Vamos a Aprender books (Books 26–27) are image-only viewer assemblies with no text layer, read from rendered pages (covers, printed contents pp. 6–7 and body spot-checks — Matemáticas 10 printed p. 110 “Teorema del seno”, Matemáticas 11 printed p. 120 “Derivada de una función en un punto”); printed page = PDF page in the sampled range. In the sections below the books are cited as Books 1–27 in the placements' order. Colombia COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its student-book rows; Book 23's rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — the guide companions Book 15 with teaching plans, activity scripts and answers and teaches no student content itself — cited as the evidence kind it is and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent for guide rows). Book errors: none material at placement grain — the Secundaria Activa Grade 9 module's contents page misprints two of its own headings (“Captítulo”, “Undiad 2”); the content is unaffected and rows use the corrected unit names. The placements' named exclusions — presentation letters, “how this book works” pages, author/imprint pages, transversal civics/health/environment themes, practice/evaluation apparatus (“Practica más”, “Evaluación del aprendizaje”, answer keys) and the Escuela Nueva “Estándares/Competencias” boxes at each unidad opening (MEN's own curriculum-standard statements; the taught content is placed at the guías themselves) — are stocked nowhere. The Círculos de Aprendizaje integrated books and the Retos para Gigantes Transición weekly guides are NOT in this set (filed as integrado, not matematicas) and are stocked nowhere. Colombia's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (27/27) has been read and placed, on the in-hand definition.

Into this lesson the placements stock 11 items from Book 10, Book 11, Book 14, Book 17, Book 19, Book 21, Book 24, Book 25, Book 26.

## Andorra added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Andorra's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **4 documents, all of them official Escola Andorrana curriculum programmes published by decree in the Butlletí Oficial del Principat d'Andorra (BOPA); 0 pupil (student) books** — and placed 679 curriculum-requirement items. A programme states what is REQUIRED to be taught; it teaches nothing itself. Every Andorra item in this section is therefore TEACHER EVIDENCE — a curriculum requirement, worded as a requirement — cited as the evidence kind it is; Andorra is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until Andorran pupil books are in hand and read. Set correction, as the placements prove it: the expansion map recorded 3 books in hand; the true set is 4 — the grades 1–6 programme (Decret 376/2021, Annex VII) is held in the GitHub repo but sits in no staging books.json, which is why the map missed it. All four programmes are Andorra's OWN (Govern d'Andorra, in Catalan); the Spanish and French systems also taught in Andorra (Spain's Anaya and Innovamat manuals; France's Le Livre Scolaire) are list-only names on official school book lists — no file is held, so they are cited nowhere in this course and credited to no country — and the Escola Andorrana itself has no commercial pupil-textbook series, so programmes are all that exist to hold. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct stated requirement — each specific competency, and each distinct entry in the programmes' learning-resource tables, tagged by cycle where the table splits by cycle — and a procedure entry that merely restates a concept entry is folded into it, not double-counted. The placements' named exclusions — the decree preambles, the introductions and competency rationale, the didactic orientations, the assessment criteria and end-of-cycle expectations, the synthesis tables (verified verbatim repeats of the competency sections), the attitudes/values lists, Book 1's glossary and opening summary table, and the Council of Europe cross-references — are stocked nowhere. Book 3's synthesis table words its C2 end-of-cycle expectation as modelling "situacions cientificotècniques" (scientific-technical) inside the social-sciences programme — a copy-over slip in the decree as published; Book 3's competency section itself is consistently social-sciences, and the slip is copied into no row below. Book 2 states its cone/polyhedron sections twice (forms table and models table, printed pp. 32–33) — counted once in the placements and stocked once. Printed page = PDF page in all four books (Books 2–4 carry the BOPA folio; Book 1's Portal PDF is paginated at the foot the same way). Into this lesson the placements stock 36 teacher-evidence items from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4.

**Book 1:** *Programa de matemàtiques de primera ensenyança* (curriculum programme, Grades 1–6) — Andorra, Govern d'Andorra, Escola Andorrana; Decret 376/2021, Annex VII; ISBN 978-99920-0-934-5; 688,455 bytes, 49 PDF pages; printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an official curriculum programme: it states what is required to be taught and teaches nothing itself.

TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an official curriculum programme: it states what is required to be taught and teaches nothing itself.

TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an official curriculum programme: it states what is required to be taught and teaches nothing itself.

Andorra enters this lesson as teacher evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count above — every book in Andorra's in-hand set is an official curriculum programme, not a pupil textbook, cited as the evidence kind it is, never counted as a pupil's book (the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent). Andorra's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): all 4 programmes are read and stocked, but no pupil textbook is in hand — none exists to hold for the Escola Andorrana — and the Spanish and French systems' maths manuals are list-only names with no files held.

Placements: liqlearns-courses-work/expansion-2026-11-10/maths/andorra-placements.md.

## Angola added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Angola's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **6 books, all pupil textbooks: Matemática, Manuais da 1.ª–6.ª Classe (Ministério da Educação — MED, Angola; updated editions of 2021, in Portuguese). There is no teacher guide in the Angola Maths set in hand, so every Angola row in this course is a pupil-textbook row — there are no teacher-evidence rows, and Angola counts in every lesson it stocks** — and placed 229 teaching items. The expansion map's “6 student + 0 teacher = 6” is correct: download-lists/angola.md, the phase3-staging checklist, textbooks.tsv, phase3-push-state.json (“pushed”) and the staging books.json files all give the same six distinct pupil textbooks, and nothing else. None of the six PDFs was on local disk (pusher-secured only), so all six were fetched fresh for the read from the books' recorded second official source, the ministry's Xilonga host, byte-exact against the records. One set nuance: the portal's 32-manual set contains no 2.ª Classe Matemática at all — the 2.ª book in hand (Book 2) exists only as an unlinked Xilonga file (MAT02-LR.pdf) found by pattern probe on 2026-10-23. A Guia Prático Matemática 6ª exists only as an exact-link reader copy and is NOT in hand — cited nowhere; if one is ever secured it enters as teacher evidence only. The Book 2 and Book 4 PDFs carry a massively duplicated text layer (the same text overprinted many times per page) — a production flaw of the files; the content is fully legible and both books were read in full from it. Twelve book errors are recorded in the placements; they are carried in the book entries below and taught corrected. The placements' named exclusions — the apresentação (ministerial foreword letter) and anthem/flag/civic front matter, the bibliografia pages, pure colouring, tracing and copying practice with no new concept, the editorial production notes printed inside the books, and exercise sets as such (exercises practise the item under which they sit) — are stocked nowhere. Printed-page bases: Books 1 and 3 — printed page = PDF page − 1; Books 2, 4, 5 and 6 — printed page = PDF page. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 items (all from pupil textbooks) from Book 5 and Book 6.

**Book 5:** *Matemática — Manual da 5.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 5) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Texto Editores, 2021, 2.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-8884-84-8; 4,730,235 bytes, 128 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book errors, taught corrected: printed p. 29's worked example 12 + 37 + 7 + 3 is printed as = 55 with a garbled regrouping (correct total: 59); printed p. 73's equivalence line prints 1/3 = 2/6 = 2/9 (the last term should be 3/9); printed p. 127's median worked example uses an ordered list containing 12, which is not in the exercise's data, and drops one 10 (the median is taught from the data as printed in the exercise: ordered 6, 7, 9, 10, 10, 10, 11, 13, 14 → median 10).

**Book 6:** *Matemática — Manual da 6.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 6) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Texto Editores, 2021, 2.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-8884-85-5; 2,544,284 bytes, 104 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book notes, taught corrected: printed p. 51 first presents the ambiguous expression 28 : 7 × 2 with two candidate readings and tells the pupil to ask the teacher — the rule is fixed by the worked examples on printed p. 52, and the course teaches the standard order (× and ÷ in order, before + and −); printed p. 62's direct-proportionality worked line prints the third ratio with denominator 6 where the sequence's term is 5 (150/5 = 30 — taught from the sequence). The book carries printer's proof marks, and one running footer misprints “Matemática 5ª Classe” (printed p. 99) — production matter, cited nowhere.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Statistics: population (finite and infinite); sample and sample size; qualitative and quantitative variables | Angola — *Matemática — Manual da 5.ª Classe* (pupil textbook), Grade 5, printed pp. 118–121 |
| Collecting data: survey, questionnaire, observation, document analysis | Angola — *Matemática — Manual da 5.ª Classe* (pupil textbook), Grade 5, printed pp. 121–123 |
| Absolute frequency and frequency tables | Angola — *Matemática — Manual da 5.ª Classe* (pupil textbook), Grade 5, printed pp. 123–125 |
| Bar charts | Angola — *Matemática — Manual da 5.ª Classe* (pupil textbook), Grade 5, printed pp. 125–126 |
| Pictograms | Angola — *Matemática — Manual da 5.ª Classe* (pupil textbook), Grade 5, printed pp. 126–127 |
| Introduction to statistics: collecting, organising and analysing data | Angola — *Matemática — Manual da 6.ª Classe* (pupil textbook), Grade 6, printed p. 74 |
| Absolute frequency and relative frequency; frequency tables with tally marks | Angola — *Matemática — Manual da 6.ª Classe* (pupil textbook), Grade 6, printed p. 75 |
| Bar charts and pictograms (revision from the 5.ª Classe) | Angola — *Matemática — Manual da 6.ª Classe* (pupil textbook), Grade 6, printed p. 76 |

Angola is added to the countries line and to the acceptance count above on its pupil-textbook rows — all six books in its set are pupil textbooks and there are no teacher-evidence rows. Angola's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (all 6) has been read and stocked.

Placements: liqlearns-courses-work/expansion-2026-11-10/maths/angola-placements.md.

## Moldova added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Moldova's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **12 books, all pupil textbooks: the Matematică / Mathematics student manual for each of Grades 1–12 (Republic of Moldova, in Romanian). There is no teacher guide and no syllabus in the Moldova Maths set in hand, so every Moldova row in this course is a pupil-textbook row — there are no teacher-evidence rows, and Moldova counts in every lesson it stocks** — and placed 394 teaching items. The expansion map's “12 student + 0 teacher” is correct: textbooks.tsv, phase3-push-state.json (“pushed”) and the staging books.json files reconcile exactly at 12 distinct works — Grades 3–12 (10 books) recorded in books.json, Grades 1–2 as git-blob files recorded in textbooks.tsv. The old closed-52 inventory's “Moldova link-only, 0 in hand” is refuted: all 12 books sit secured in the tensae-code/liqlearns-textbooks GitHub repo and were read from there for this expansion (Grades 3, 4 and 6–12 via the mid-size-books-8 release; Grades 1, 2 and 5 via the Contents API on their textbooks.tsv git-blob paths), byte-verified — MD5s match books.json exactly for the ten Grades 3–12 books. The Grades 10–12 books are dual-track (profil real / profil umanistic) throughout; each section is stocked once, as one Moldova row, not once per track. Book errors: none found that changes a placement; the Grade 9 file's PDF p. 178 carries a stray page from another book (a biology text page) bound after the back cover in the publisher's print file — a file artifact, not part of the mathematics manual, cited nowhere. The placements' named exclusions — the “Cine seamănă culege” review blocks, the Recapitulare / sistematizare / evaluare sections, the “Să recapitulăm” summaries, the recap exercise sets and “Test sumativ” pages closing chapters, and the “Răspunsuri (și indicații)” answer keys — are stocked nowhere, except where a review section is a topic's only statement, which is then marked as such. Printed-page bases: Book 2 — printed page = PDF page − 1; Book 6 — printed page = PDF page − 1; Book 12 — printed pages as listed in the book's own Cuprins. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items (all from pupil textbooks) from Book 2, Book 6 and Book 12.

**Book 2:** *Mathematics* (pupil textbook, Grade 2) — Moldova, Ministerul Educației al Republicii Moldova, Editura Prut Internațional, re-edition approved by Order nr. 544 din 8 iunie 2015; 24,017,939 bytes, 138 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page − 1).

**Book 6:** *Matematică: Manual pentru clasa a VI-a* (pupil textbook, Grade 6) — Moldova, MECC; curriculum Order nr. 906/2019; approved Order nr. 1343 din 03 decembrie 2020; 6,364,545 bytes, 246 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page − 1).

**Book 12:** *Matematică: Manual pentru clasa a XII-a* (pupil textbook, Grade 12) — Moldova, MECC; 20,690,648 bytes, 266 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed pages as listed in the book's own Cuprins).

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Organising data in tables | Moldova — *Mathematics* (pupil textbook), Grade 2, printed p. 82 |
| Elements of organising data | Moldova — *Matematică: Manual pentru clasa a VI-a* (pupil textbook), Grade 6, printed p. 186 |
| Fundamental statistical notions; recording and grouping data | Moldova — *Matematică: Manual pentru clasa a XII-a* (pupil textbook), Grade 12, printed pp. 114–115 |
| Graphical representation of statistical data | Moldova — *Matematică: Manual pentru clasa a XII-a* (pupil textbook), Grade 12, printed p. 120 |

Moldova is added to the countries line and to the acceptance count above on its pupil-textbook rows — all twelve books in its set are pupil textbooks and there are no teacher-evidence rows. Moldova's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (all 12) has been read and stocked.
